caturviṃśatsamā rājā vaṃśakastu bhaviṣyati
udāsī bhavitā tasmāttrayastriṃśatsamānṛpaḥ
catvāriṃśatsamābhāvyo rājā vai nandivarddhanaḥ
catvāriṃśattrayaścaiva mahānandī bhaviṣyati
ityete bhavitāro vai daśa dvau śiśunākajāḥ
śatāni trīṇipūrṇāni ṣaṣṭivarṣādhikāni tu
śiśunākā bhaviṣyanti rājānaḥ kṣatrabandhavaḥ
etaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhaviṣyanti yāvatkalinṛpāḥ pare
caturviṃśattathaikṣvākāḥ pāñcālāḥ saptaviśatiḥ
kāśeyāstu caturviṃśadaṣṭāviṃśattu haihayāḥ
kaliṅgāścaiva dvātriṃśadaśmakāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
kuruvaścāpi ṣaṭviṃśadaṣṭāviṃśastu maithilāḥ
śūrasenāstrayoviṃśadvītihotrāśca viśatiḥ
ete sarve bhaviṣyanti ekakālaṃ mahīkṣitaḥ
mahānandisutaścāpi śūdrāyāṃ kalikāṃśajaḥ
tataḥ prabhṛti rājāno bhaviṣyāḥ śūdrayonayaḥ
aṣṭāśīti tu varṣāṇi pṛthivyāñca bhaviṣyati
sukalpādi sutāhyaṣṭau samādvādaśate nṛpāḥ
mahāpadmasya paryyāye bhaviṣyanti nṛpāḥ kramāt
uddhariṣyati kauṭilyaḥ samairdvādaśabhiḥ sutān
bhuktvā mahīṃ varṣaśataṃ tato mauryyān gamiṣyati
bhavitā śatadhanvā ca tasya putrastu ṣaṭsamāḥ
ṣaṭtriṃśattu samārājā bhavitā śaka eva ca
saptānāṃ daśavarṣāṇi tasya naptā bhaviṣyati
rājā daśaratho'ṣṭau tu tasya putro bhaviṣyati
bhavitā navavarṣāṇi tasya putraśca saptatiḥ
saptatriṃśacchataṃ pūrṇaṃ tebhyaḥ śuṅgan gamiṣyati
kārayiṣyati vai rājyaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśati samānṛpaḥ
bhavitāpi vasujyeṣṭhaḥ saptavarṣāṇi vai nṛpaḥ
vasumitrastathā bhāvyo daśavarṣāṇi vai tataḥ
dvātriṃśattu samābhāgaḥ samābhāgāttato nṛpaḥ
daśaite kṣudrarājāno bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasundharām
śataṃ pūrṇaṃ śate dveca tataḥ śuṅgān gamiṣyati
devabhūmimathotsādya śauṅgastu bhavitā nṛpaḥ
bhaviṣyati samārājā navakāṇvāyano nṛpaḥ
nārāyaṇaḥ sutastasya bhavitā dvādaśaiva tu
suśarmā tatsutaścāpi bhaviṣyati daśaiva tu
ityete śuṅgabhṛtyāstu smṛtā kāṇvāyanānṛpāḥ
catvāriṃśaddvijā hyete kāṇvā bokṣyanti vai mahīm
catvāriṃśatpañca caiva bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasundharām
ete praṇatasāmantā bhaviṣyā dhārmikāśca ye
yeṣāṃ paryyāyakāle tu bhūmirāndhrān gamiṣyati
sūta uvāca
kāṇvāyanāstato bhūpāḥ suśarmāṇaḥ prasahyatām
śuṅgānāñcaiva yaccheṣaṃ kṣapitvā tu balīyasaḥ
śiśukondhraḥ sajātīyaḥ prāpsyatīmāṃ vasundharām
trayoviṃśat samārājā śiśukastu bhaviṣyati
pūrṇotsaṃgastato rājā varṣāṇyaṣṭādaśaiva tu
pañcāśataṃ samāḥ ṣaṭca śāntakarṇirbhaviṣyati
daśacāṣṭau ca varṣāṇi tasya lambodaraḥ sutaḥ
daśacāṣṭau ca varṣāṇi medhasvātirbhaviṣyati
svātiśca bhavitā rājā samāstvaṣṭādaśaiva tu
kuntalaḥ svātikarṇastu bhavitāṣṭau samānṛpaḥ
ekasaṃvatsaraṃ rājā svātivarṇo bhaviṣyati
bhavitā riktavarṇastu varṣāṇi pañcaviṃśati
tataḥ saṃvatsarān pañca hālo rājā bhaviṣyati
pañcamandulako rājā bhaviṣyati samā nṛpa
cakoraḥ svātikarṇastu ṣaṇmāsān vai bhaviṣyati
aṣṭāviṃśati varṣāṇi śivasvātirbhaviṣyati
rājā ca gautamī putro hyekaviṃśatyato nṛpaḥ
aṣṭāviṃśatisutastasya sulomā vai bhaviṣyati
śivaśrīrvai sulomāttu saptaiva bhavitā nṛpaḥ
śivaskandha śāntikarṇād bhavitā hyātmajaḥ samāḥ
navaviṃśati varṣāṇi yajñaḥ śrī śāntikarṇikaḥ
caṇḍaśrīḥ śāntikarṇastu tasya putra samādaśa
sulomā saptavarṣāṇi anyasteṣāṃ bhaviṣyati
teṣāṃ varṣaśatāni syuḥ catvāri ṣaṣṭireva ca
āndhrāṇāṃ saṃsthitā rājye teṣāṃ bhṛtyānvaye nṛpāḥ
saptaivāndhrā bhaviṣyanti daśābhīrāstathā nṛpāḥ
saptagardabhilāścāpi śakāścāṣṭādaśaiva tu
yavanāṣṭau bhaviṣyanti tuṣārāśca caturdaśa
trayodaśa guruṃḍāśca hūṇāhyekonaviṃśātiḥ
yavanāṣṭau bhaviṣyanti saptāśīti mahīmimām
saptagarddabhilābhūyo bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasundharām
saptavarṣasahasrāṇi tuṣārāṇāṃ mahī smṛtā
śatāni trīṇyaśītiñca śatānyaṣṭādaśaiva tu
śatānyarddhañcatuṣkāṇi bhavitavyāstrayodaśa
guruṇḍā vṛṣalaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhokṣyante mlecchasambhavāḥ
śatāni trīṇi bhokṣyante varṣāṇyekādaśaiva tu
āndhrāḥ śrīpārvvatīyāśca te dvipañcāśataṃ samāḥ
saptaṣaṣṭistu varṣāṇi daśābhīrāstathaiva ca
teṣūtsanneṣu kālena tataḥ kilakilānṛpāḥ
tairvimiśrā janapadā āryyāmlecchāśca sarvaśaḥ
lubdhānṛtabruvāścaiva bhavitāro nṛpāstathā
kalkinānihatāḥ sarve āryyā mlecchāśca sarvataḥ
adhārmikāścaye'pyarthaṃ pāṣaṇḍāścaiva sarvaśaḥ
praṇaṣṭe nṛpavaṃśe tu sandhyā śiṣṭe kalau yuge
kiñcicchiṣṭāḥ prajāstā vai dharme naṣṭe'parigrahāḥ
asādhavo hyasatvāśca vyādhiśokena pīḍitāḥ
anāvṛṣṭihatāścaiva paraspara vadhepsavaḥ
aśaraṇyāḥ paritrastāḥ saṅkaṭaṃ ghoramāśritāḥ
patramūlaphalāhārāḥ cīrapatrājināmbarāḥ
evaṃ kaṣṭamanuprāptāḥ prajākāle yugāntake
niḥśeṣāstu bhaviṣyanti sārddhaṃ kaliyugena tu
sasandhyāṃśe suniḥ śeṣe kṛtaṃ tu pratipatsyate
evaṃ vaṃśakramaḥ kṛtsnaḥ kīrtito yo mayā kramāt
atītā varttamānāśca tathaivānāgatāśca ye
mahāpadmābhiṣekāttu yāvajjanmaparīkṣitaḥ
evaṃ varṣasahasrantu jñeyaṃ pañcāśaduttaram
anantaraṃ śatānyaṣṭau ṣaṭtriṃśattu samāstathā
tāvatkālāntaraṃ bhāvyamāndhrāntādāparīkṣitaḥ
bhaviṣyete prasaṅkhyātāḥ purāṇajñaiḥ śrutarṣibhiḥ
saptarṣayastadā prāṃśu pradīptenāgninā samāḥ
saptaviṃśati bhāvyānāṃ āndhrāṇāntu yadā punaḥ
saptarṣayastu tiṣṭhanti paryyāyeṇa śataṃ śatam
saptarṣīṇāmuparye tat smṛtaṃ vai divyasaṃjñayā
samādivyāḥ smṛtā ṣaṣṭirdivyābdāni tu saptabhiḥ
saptarṣīṇāñca yau pūrvau dṛśyete hyuditau niśi
tena saptarṣayo jñeyā yuktā vyomni śataṃ samāḥ
nakṣatrāṇāmṛṣīṇāñca yogasyaitannidarśanam
saptarṣayo maghāyuktāḥ kāle pārikṣite śatam
brāhmaṇastu caturviṃśā bhaviṣyati śataṃ samāḥ
anṛtopahatā lubdhā dharmataḥ kāmato'rthataḥ
śrautasmārteti śithile naṣṭavarṇāśrame tathā
saṅkaraṃ durbalātmānaḥ pratipatsyanti mohitāḥ
brāhmaṇāḥ śūdrayonisthāḥ śūdrā vai mantrayonayaḥ
krameṇaiva ca dṛśyante svavarṇāntaradāyakam
kṣayameva gamiṣyanti kṣīṇaśeṣā yugakṣaye
yasminkṛṣṇo divaṃ yātastasminneva tadāhani
pratipannaṃ kaliyugaṃ pramāṇaṃ tasya me śrṛṇu
catuḥ śatasahasrantu varṣāṇāṃ vai smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ
catvāryaṣṭasahasrāṇi saṅkhyātaṃ mānuṣeṇa tu
niḥśeṣetu tadā tasmin kṛtaṃ vai pratipatsyate
ailaścekṣvākuvaṃśaśca sahadevaḥ prakīrttitāḥ
ikṣvākoḥ saṃsmṛtaṃ kṣatraṃ sumitrāntaṃ bhaviṣyati
ailaṃ kṣatraṃsamākrāntaṃ somavaṃśavido viduḥ
ete vivasvataḥ putrāḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ
atītā vartamānāśca tathaivānāgatāśca ye
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāstathā śūdrāśca vai smṛtāḥ
devāpiḥ pauravo rājā aikṣvāko yaśca te mataḥ
navaviṃśe yuge so vai vaṃśasyādirbhaviṣyati
devāpiputraḥ satyastu ailānāṃ bhavitā nṛpaḥ
evaṃ sarveṣu vijñeyaṃ santānārthantu lakṣaṇam
kṣīṇe kaliyugecaiva tiṣṭhantīti kṛte yuge
evamevaṃ tu sarveṣu tiṣyānteṣvantareṣu ca
saptarṣayo nṛpaiḥ sārddhaṃ santānārthaṃ yuge yuge
manvantarāṇāṃ santāne santānāśca śrutau smṛtāḥ
yathā praśāntisteṣāṃ vai prakṛtīnāṃ yathākṣayaḥ
saptarṣayo vidusteṣāṃ dīrghāyustvaṃ kṣayodayau
etena kramayogena ailā ikṣvākavo nṛpāḥ
utpadyamānāstretāyāṃ kṣīyamāṇāḥ kalau yuge
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa kṣatreniravaśeṣite
dvivaṃśakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ kīrtayiṣye nibodha me
ailañcekṣvākuvaṃśañca prakṛtiṃ paricakṣate
rājānaḥ śreṇibaddhāśca tathānte kṣatriyā bhuvi
ailavaṃśāstu bhūyāṃso na tathekṣvākavo nṛpāḥ
eṣāmekaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ kulānāmabhirocate
tāvadeva tu bhojānāṃ vistārād dviguṇaṃ smṛtam
bhojānāṃ dviguṇāṃ kṣatraṃ caturddhā tadyathā tatham
te hyatītāḥ sa nāmāno bruvatastānnibodha me
śataṃ vai prativindhyānāṃ śataṃ nāgāḥ śataṃ hayāḥ
śatamekaṃ dhārtarāṣṭrā hyaśītirjanamejayāḥ
śataṃ vai brahmadattānāṃ vīrāṇāṃ kuravaḥ śatam
tataḥ śatañca pañcālāḥ śataṃ kāśikuśādayaḥ
tathāpare sahasre dve ye nīpāḥ śaśabindavaḥ
iṣṭavantaśca te sarve sarve niyutadakṣiṇāḥ
evaṃ rājarṣayo'tītāḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ
teṣāṃ te nidhanotpattau lokasaṃsthitayaḥ sthitāḥ
aṣṭāviṃśasamākhyātā gatā vaivasvate'ntare
aite devagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ śiṣṭā ye tānnibodhita
catvāriṃśattrayaścaiva bhaviṣyāste mahātmanaḥ
avaśiṣṭā yugākhyāste tato vaivasvato hyayam
etadvaḥ rājarṣayo ye tu atītāste yugaiḥ saha
ye te yayāti vaṃśyānāṃ ye ca vaṃśā viśāmpate
āyuḥ kīrtiṃ dhanaṃ svargaṃ putravāṃścābhijāyate
dhāraṇācchravaṇāccaiva paraṃ svargasya dhīmataḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
nyāyenārjanamarthānāṃ varddhanañcābhirakṣaṇam
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi mahādānānukīrtanam
dānadharme'pi yannoktaṃ viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇāṃ duḥsvapna nāśanam
yattat ṣoḍaśadā proktaṃ vāsudevena bhūtale
puṇyaṃ pavitramāyuṣyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham
pūjitaṃ devatābhiśca brahmaviṣṇu śivādibhiḥ
ādyantu sarvadānānāṃ tulā puruṣasaṃjñakam
hiraṇyagarbhadānañca brahmāṇḍaṃ tadanantaram
hiraṇyakāmadhenuśca hiraṇyāśvastathaiva ca
pañcalāṅgalakaṃ tadvad dharādānaṃ tathaiva ca
mahābhūtaghaṭastadvat ṣoḍaśaṃ parikīrtitam
sarvāṇyetāni kṛtavān purā śamvarasūdanaḥ
vāsudevastu bhagavān ambarīṣo'tha bhārgavaḥ
kārtavīryyārjuno nāma prahlādaḥ pṛthureva ca
kuryuranye mahīpālāḥ kecicca bharatādayaḥ
yasmādvighnasahasreṇa mahādānāni sarvadā
eṣāmanyatamaṃ kuryyādvāsudeva prasādataḥ
mahādānamukhaṃ kuryyādvipraiścaivānumoditaḥ
etadevāha manave paripṛṣṭo janārdanaḥ
yathāvadanuvakṣyāmi śrṛṇudhvamṛṣisattamāḥ
mahādānāni yānīha pavitrāṇi śubhāni ca
yāni noktāni guhyāni mahādānāni ṣoḍaśa
tāni te kathayiṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ
tulāpuruṣa yāgo'yaṃ yeṣāmādau vidhīyate
yugādiṣu parāgeṣu tathā manvantarādiṣu
saṅkrāntau tau vaidhṛtidine caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca
sitapañcadaśīparva dvādaśīṣvaṣṭakāsu ca
yajñotsavavivāheṣu duḥsvapnādbhutadarśane
dravyabrāhmaṇalābhe vā śraddhā vā yatra jāyate
tīrthe vāyatane goṣṭe kūpārāmasaritsu ca
gṛhe vāyatane vāpi taḍāge rucire tathā
mahādānāni deyāni saṃsārabhayabhīruṇā
anityaṃ jīvitaṃ yasmāt vasucātīva cañcalam
keśeṣvevagṛhītaḥ san mṛtyunādharmamācaret
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
ṣoḍaśāratnimātrantu daśadvādaśavākarān
maṇḍapaṃ kārayedvidvān caturbhadrāsanaṃ budhaḥ
tanmadhye toraṇaṃ kuryyāt sāradārumayaṃ budhaḥ
kuryyāt kuṇḍāni catvāri caturdikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ
samekhalā yoni yutāni kuryāt sampūrṇakumbhāni sahāsanāni
sutāmrapātradvayasaṃyutāni sayajñapātrāṇi suviṣṭarāṇi
hastapramāṇāni tilājya dhūpa puṣpopahārāṇi suśobhanāni
atrārcanaṃ brahmaśivācyutānāṃ tatraiva kāryyaṃ phalamālyavastraiḥ
lokeśavarṇāḥ paritaḥ patākā madhye dhvajaḥ kiṅkiṇikāyutaḥ syāt
dvāreṣu kāryāṇi ca toraṇāni catvāryyapi kṣīravanaspatīnām
śāleṅgudīcandanadevadāruśrīparṇibilvapriyakāñcanottham
stambhadvayaṃ hastayugāvakhātaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ pañcakarocchritañca
samānajātiśca tulāvalambhyā haimena madhye puruṣeṇa yuktā
dairghyeṇa sā hastacatuṣṭayaṃ syāt pṛthutvamasyāstu daśāṃgulāni
suvarṇapaṭṭābharaṇā tu kāryyā sā lohapāśadvayaśrṛṅgalābhiḥ
yutā suvarṇena tu ratnamālā vibhūṣitāmālyavilepanābhyām
cakraṃ likhedvārijagarbhayuktaṃ nānārajobhiḥ bhuvipuṣpakīrṇam
vimānakañcopari pañcavarṇaṃ saṃsthāpayet puṣpaphalopaśobham
athartvijo vedavidaśca kāryyāḥ surūpaveśānvayaśīlayuktāḥ
vidhānadakṣāḥ paṭavo'nukūlā ye cāryyadeśaprabhavā dvijendrāḥ
guruśca vedāntavidāryyavaṃśa samudbhavaḥ śīlakulābhirūpaḥ
purāṇaśāstrābhirato'tidakṣaḥ prasannagambhīrasarasvatīkaḥ
sitāmbaraḥ kuṇḍalahemasūtrakeyūrakaṇṭābharaṇābhirāmaḥ
pūrveṇa ṛgvedavidāvathāstāṃ yajurvidau dakṣiṇataśca śastau
vināyakādigrahalokapālavasvaṣṭakādityamarudgaṇānām
brahmācyute sārkaṃvanaspatīnāṃ svamantrato homacatuṣṭayaṃ syāt
japyāni sūktāni tathaiva caiṣāmanukrameṇāpi yathā svarūpam
homāvasāne kṛtatūryyanādo gururgṛhītvā balipuṣpadhūpam
śubhāśubhānandaśucāmadhīśa śivāya naḥ pāhi makhaṃ namaste
ehyehi rakṣogaṇanāyakastvaṃ sarvaistu betālapiśācasaṅghaiḥ
ehyehi yādogaṇavāridhīnāṅgaṇena parjanyamahāpsarobhiḥ
ehyehi yajñe mama rakṣaṇāya mṛgādhirūḍhaḥ mahasiddhasaṅghaiḥ
prāṇādhipaḥ kālakaveḥ sahāyaḥ gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
ehyehi yajñeśvara yajñarakṣāṃ vidhatsva nakṣatragaṇena sārddham
sarvauṣadhībhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ sahaiva gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
ehyehi viśveśvara nastriśūlakapālakhaṭvāṅgadhareṇa sārddham
lokeśayajñeśvara yajñasiddhyai gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
ehyehi pātāladharādharendra nāgāṅganākinnaragīyamāna
trailokye yāni bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca
brahmaviṣṇuśivaiḥ sārddhaṃ rakṣāṃ kurvantu tāni me
devadānavagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ
sarve mamādhvare rakṣāṃ prakurvantu mudānvitāḥ
ityāvāhya surāndadyādṛtvigbhyo hemabhūṣaṇam
kuṇḍalānica haimāni sūtrāṇi kaṭakānica
aṃgulīyapavitrāṇi vāsāṃsi śayanāni ca
dviguṇaṃ gurave dadyād bhūṣaṇācchādanāni ca
japeyuḥ śāntikādhyāyaṃ jāpakāḥ sarvatodiśam
ādāvanteca madhye ca kuryyād brāhmaṇavācanam
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
śuklamālyāmbaro bhūtvā tāṃ tulāmabhimantrayet
namaste sarvadevānāṃ śaktistvaṃ satyamāsthitā
sākṣibhūtā jagaddhātrī nirmitā viśvayoninā
ekataḥ sarvasatyāni tathānṛtaśatāni ca
tvaṃ tule sarvabhūtānāṃ pramāṇamiha kīrtitā
māṃ tolayantī saṃsārāduddharasva namo'stute
yo'sau tatvādhipo devaḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ
tvaṃ hare tārayasvāsmānnasmāt saṃsārakardamāt
puṇyakālaṃ samāsādya kṛtvaivamadhivāsanam
punaḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā tulāmārohayed budhaḥ
sa khaḍgacarmakavacaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
dharmarājamathādāya haimaṃ sūryyeṇa saṃyutam
karābhyāṃ baddhamuṣṭibhyāmāste paśyan harermukham
samādabhyadhikaṃ yāvat kāñcanaṃ cātinirmalam
puṣṭikāmastu kurvīta bhūmisaṃsthaṃ nareśvaraḥ
kṣaṇamātraṃ tataḥ sthitvā punarevamudīrayet
namaste sarvabhūtānāṃ sākṣibhūte sanātani
pitāmahena devi tvaṃ nirmitā parameṣṭinā
prāpya teṣāmanujñāṃ tu tathānyebhyo'pi dāpayet
dīnānātha viśiṣṭādīn pūjayed brāhmaṇaiḥ saha
na ciraṃ dhārayed gehe suvarṇaṃ prokṣitaṃ budhaḥ
tiṣṭed bhayāvahaṃ yasmācchokavyādhikaraṃ nṛṇām
śīghraṃ parasvīkaraṇācchreyaḥ prāpnoti mānavaḥ
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
pūjyamāno'psarobhiśca tato viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet
karmakṣayādiha punarbhuvi rājarājo bhūpālamaulimaṇirañcitapādapīṭhaḥ
yo vā śrṛṇoti paṭhatīndrasamānarūpaḥ prāpnoti dhāma sapurandaradevajuṣṭam
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
nāmnā hiraṇyagarbhākhyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyaṃ dinamathāsādya tulā puruṣadānavat
ṛtvig maṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādānādikam
kuryyādupoṣitastadvat lokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā tadvat kṛtvādhivāsanam
brāhmaṇairānayetkumbhaṃ tapanīyamayaṃ śubham
tribhāgahīnavistāramājyakṣīrābhipūritam
daśāstrāṇi ca ratnāni dātrīṃ sūcīṃ tathaiva ca
hemanālaṃ sapiṭhakaṃ bahirāditya saṃyutam
tathaivāvaraṇaṃ nābherupavītañca kāñcanam
pārśvataḥ sthāpayettadvat haimadaṇḍakamaṇḍalū
padmākāraṃ pidhānaṃ syāt samantādaṃgulādhikam
śuklamālyāmbaradharaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
imamuccārayen mantraṃ gṛhīta kusumāñjaliḥ
brahmādayastathā devāḥ namaste viśvadhāriṇe
tasmān māmuddharāśeṣa duḥkhasaṃsārasāgarāt
evamāmantrya tanmadhyamāviśyāsta udṅmukhaḥ
muṣṭibhyāṃ parisaṃgṛhya dharmarājacaturmukhau
jānumadhye śiraḥ kṛtvā tiṣṭeducchvāsapañcakam
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā
gītamaṅgalaghoṣeṇa gururutthāpayet tataḥ
jātakarmādikāḥ kuryuḥ kriyāḥ ṣoḍaśa cāparāḥ
caturbhiḥ kalaśairbhūyaḥ tataste dvijapuṅgavāḥ
snāpayeyuḥ prasannāgāḥ sarvābharayaṇabhūṣitāḥ
divyenānena vapuṣā ciraṃ jīva sukhī bhava
te pūjyāḥ sarvabhāvena bahavo vā tadājñayā
grāmaṃ vā viṣayaṃ vāpi yadanyadapi sambhavet
pureṣu lokapālānāṃ pratimanvantaraṃ vaset
matimapi ca janānāṃ yo dadāti priyārthaṃ vibudhapatijanānāṃ nāyakaḥ syādamogham
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi brahmāṇḍavidhimuttamam
yacchreṣṭhaṃ sarvadānānāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyaṃ dinamathāsādya tulā puruṣa dānavat
ṛtvigmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryyādadhivāsanakaṃ tathā
kuryyādviṃśapalādūrdhvamāsahasrācca śaktitaḥ
kalaśadvayasaṃyuktaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ kāñcanaṃ budhaḥ
diggajāṣṭakasaṃyuktaṃ ṣaḍvedāṅga samanvitam
śivācyutārkaśikharamumā lakṣmī samanvitam
vitasteraṃ gulaśataṃ yāvadāyāma vistaram
tathāṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt parikalpayet
pūrveṇānantaśayanaṃ pradyumnaṃ pūrvadakṣiṇe
prakṛtiṃ dakṣiṇe deśe saṅkarṣaṇamataḥ param
samantād guḍapīṭhasthānarcayet kāñcanān budhaḥ
sthāpayedvastrasamvītān pūrṇakumbhān daśaiva tu
daśaiva dhenavo deyāḥ sahaivāmbaradohanāḥ
pādukopānahacchatra cāmarāsanadarpaṇaiḥ
bhakṣyabhojyānnadī pekṣu phalamālyānulepanaiḥ
homādhivāsanānte ca snāpito vedapuṅgavaiḥ
imamuccārayenmantraṃ triḥ kṛtvātha pradakṣiṇam
saptarṣilokāmarabhūtaleśa garbheṇa sārdhaṃ vitarābhirakṣām
ye duḥkhitāste sukhino bhavantu prayāntu pāpāni carācarāṇām
tvaddānaśastrāhatapātakānāṃ brahmāṇḍadoṣaḥ pralayaṃ vrajantu
brahmāṇḍadānaśakalīkṛtapātakaughamānandayecca jananīkulamapyaśeṣam
iti paṭhati śruṇoti vā ya etat surabhavaneṣu gṛheṣu dhārmikāṇām
mahādānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapātakanāśanam
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā lokeśāvāhanaṃ tathā
ṛtvijjhaḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kāñcanaṃ kārayedbudhaṃ nānāphalasamanvitam
nānāvihagavacchrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca kārayet
śaktitāñcipalākūdhvamāsahasraṃ prakalpayet
brahmaviṣṇuśiropetaṃ pañcaśākhaṃ sabhāskaram
saṃtānaṃ pūrvatastadvṛtturīyāṃśena kalpayet
mandāraṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge śriyāṃ sārdhaṃ ghṛtopari
paścime pāribhedaṃ tu sāviḍyā saha jairuke
surabhīsaṃyutaṃ tadvaliṣṭheṣu haricandanam
tūrīyāṃśena kurvīta saumyena phalasaṃyutam
kauśeyavajrasaṃvītānikṣumālyaphalānvitān
tathā'ṣṭau pūrṇakalaśānpādukāṃśanabhājanam
phalamālyayutaṃ tadvadupariṣṭādvitānakam
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantātparikalpayet
homādhivāsanānte ca snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mantrametamudīrayet
viśvaṃbharāya devāya namaste viśvamūrtaye
yasmātsvameva viśvātmā brahmā sthāṇurdivākaraḥ
dhṛtārmūrtaparaṃ bījamataḥ pāhi sanātanam
saṃtānādyairupetaḥ sanpāhi saṃsārasāgarāt
catubhyaścātha ṛtvigbhyaḥ saṃtānādīnprakalpayet
apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaḥ siddhacāraṇakirnaraiḥ
bhūtānmajjyāṃśca manujāṃstārayeddoṣasaṃyutān
stūyamāno divaḥ pṛṣṭha putrapautraśrapautrakaiḥ
vimānenārkavarṇaiḥ viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati
divi kalpaśataṃ tiṣṭhedvājarājo bhavettataḥ
nārāyaṇabalopeto nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ
nārāyaṇakathāsakto nārāyaṇapuraṃ vrajet
yo vā paṭhetsakalakalpatarūpadānaṃ yo vā śṛṇoti puruṣo'lpadhanaḥ smaredvā
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
puṇyāṃ tithiṃ samāsādya yugamanvantarādikīm
payovrataṃ trirātraṃ syādekarātramathāpi vā
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāt tulā puruṣadānavat
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kuryāddhomaḥ kāryastathaiva ca
gosahasraṃ bahiḥ kuryād vastramālya vibhūṣaṇam
suvarṇaśrṛṅgābharaṇaṃ raupyapāda samanvitam
antaḥ praveśya daśakaṃ vastramālyaiśca pūjayet
suvarṇaghaṇṭikāyuktaṃ kāṃsya dohanakānvitam
suvarṇatilakopetaṃ hemapaṭṭairalaṅkṛtam
gavāṃ daśaka madhye syāt kāñcano nandikeśvaraḥ
kauśeyavastrasamvīto nānābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
lavaṇadromaśikhare mālayekṣu phalasaṃyutaḥ
kuryāt palaśatādūrdhvaṃ sarvametadaśeṣataḥ
gośate'pi daśāṃśena sarvametat samācaret
puṇyakālaṃ samāsādya gītamaṅgala niḥsvanaiḥ
imamuccārayen mantraṃ gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
lokādhivāsinībhyaśca rohiṇībhyo namo namaḥ
gavāmaṅgeṣu tiṣṭhanti bhuvanānyekaviṃśatiḥ
brahmādayastathā devā rohiṇyaḥ pāntu mātaraḥ
gāvo me agrataḥ santu gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhata eva ca
gāvaḥ śirasi me nityaṃ gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham
yasmāttvaṃ vṛṣarūpeṇa dharma eva sanātanaḥ
aṣṭamūrteradhiṣṭānamataḥ pāhi sanātana
sarvopakaraṇopetaṃ goyutañca vicakṣaṇaḥ
gavāñca śatamekaikaṃ tadarddhaṃ vātha viṃśatim
daśa pañcātha vā dadyādanyebhyastadanujñayā
payovrataḥ punastiṣṭedekāhaṃ gosahasradaḥ
śrāvayecchṛṇuyādvāpi mahādānānukīrtanam
taddine brahmacārī syāt yadīcchedvipulāṃ śriyam
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
sarveṣāṃ lokapālānāṃ loke saṃpūjyate'maraiḥ
śatamekottarantadvat pitṝṇāṃ tārayed budhaḥ
mātāmahānāṃ tadvacca putrapautrasamanvitaḥ
yāvatkalpaśatantiṣṭet rājarājo bhavet punaḥ
aśvamedhaśataṃ kuryācchivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ
saṃsārasāgarādasmādyo'smān santārayiṣyati
anubhavati mudaṃ vā mucyamāno nikāmaṃ
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kāmadenu vidhaṃ param
sarvakāmapradaṃ nṛṇāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ tadvaddhomaḥ kāryo'dhivāsanam
tulā puruṣavat kuryāt kuṇḍamaṇḍapavedikam
kanīyasī tadardhena kāmadhenuḥ prakīrtitāḥ
śaktitastripalādūrdhvamaśakto'pīha kārayet
vedyāṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ nyasya guḍaprastha samanvitam
nyasedupari tāṃ dhenuṃ mahāratnairalaṅkṛtām
kumbhāṣṭakasamopetāṃ nānāphala samanvitām
tathāṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantāt parikalpayet
ikṣudaṇḍāṣṭakaṃ tadvannānāphala samanvitam
bhājanañcāsanaṃ tadvat tāmradohanakaṃ tathā
kauśeyavastradvayasaṃyutāṅgāṃ dīpātapatrābharaṇābhirāmām
sacāmarāṃ kuṇḍalinī saghaṇṭāṃ suvarṇaśrṛṅgīṃ parirūpyapādām
rasaiśca sarvaiḥ parito'bhijuṣṭāṃ haridrayā puṣpaphalairanekaiḥ
snātastatomaṅgalavedaghoṣaiḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sapuṣpahastaḥ
āvāhayettāṃ guruṇoktamantraiḥ dvijāya dadyādatha darbhapāṇiḥ
tvaddānaśastraśakalīkṛtapāpakaughaḥ prāpto'smi nirvṛttimatīva parāṃ namāmi
saṃsāraduḥkhaśamanāya yatasva kāmaṃ tvāṃ kāmadhenumiti devagaṇā vadanti
āmantrya śīlakularūpaguṇānvitāya viprāya yaḥ kanakadhenumimāṃ pradadyāt
prāpnoti dhāma sa purandaradevajuṣṭaṃ kanyāgaṇaiḥ parivṛtaḥ padamindumauleḥ
athāta saṃpravakṣyāmi hiraṇyāśvavidhiṃ param
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāt tulā puruṣadānavat
ṛtvikmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
pādukopānahacchatra cāmarāsanabhājanaiḥ
pūrṇakumbhāṣṭakopetaṃ mālyekṣuphalasaṃyutam
śayyāṃ sopaskarāṃ tadvat hemamārtaṇḍasaṃyutām
tataḥ sarvauṣadhī snāna snāpito dvijapuṅgavaiḥ
imamuccārayen mantraṃ gṛhīta kusumāñjaliḥ
vājirūpeṇa māmasmāt pāhi saṃsārasāgarāt
yasmād bhāsayase lokānataḥ pāhi sanātana
datvā pāpakṣayād bhānoḥ lokamabhyeti śāśvatam
sarvaṃ śayyādikaṃ datvā bhuñjītā tailameva hi
purāṇaśravaṇaṃ tadvat kārayed bhojanādikam
imaṃ hiraṇyāśvavidhiṃ karoti yaḥ saṃpūjyamāno divi devasaṅghaiḥ
yo vā śrṛṇoti puruṣo'lpadhanaḥ smaredvā hemāśvadānamabhinandayatīha loke
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
puṇyamaśvarathaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyaṃ dinamathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāt tulā puruṣadānavat
ṛtvikmaṇḍapasambhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kṛṣṇājine tilān kṛtvā kāñcanaṃ sthāpayet ratham
aṣṭāśvaṃ caturaśvaṃ vā catuścakraṃ sakūbaram
lokapālāṣṭakaṃ tadvat padmarāgadalānvitam
caturaḥ pūrṇakalaśān dhānyānyaṣṭādaśaiva tu
kauśeyavastra saṃyuktamupariṣṭādvitānakam
mālyekṣuphalasaṃyuktaṃ puruṣeṇa samanvitam
ābhārāttripalādūrdhvaṃ śaktitaḥ kārayed budhaḥ
aśvāṣṭakena saṃyuktaṃ caturbhiratha vājibhiḥ
puṇyakālamathāvāpya pūrvavatsnāpitau dvijaiḥ
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
śuklamālyāmbaro dadyādimaṃ mantramudīrayet
namo namaḥ pāpavināśanāya viśvātmane vedaturaṅgamāya
dhāmnāmadhīśāya divākarāya pāpaughadāvānala dehi śāntim
vasvaṣṭakādityamarudgaṇānāṃ tvameva dhātā paramaṃ nidhānam
dedīpyamānavapuṣāṃ vijitaprabhāvamākramya maṇḍalamakhaṇḍitacaṇḍabhānoḥ
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi hemahastirathaṃ śubham
yasya pradānād bhuvanaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yāti mānavaḥ
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya tulā puraṣa dānavat
vipravācanakaṃ kuryāl lokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
ṛtvikmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kuryāt puṣparathākāraṃ kāñcanaṃ maṇimaṇḍitam
kṛṣṇājine tiladroṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃsthāpayet ratham
lokapālāṣṭakopetaṃ brahmārkaśivasaṃyutam
madhye nārāyaṇopataṃ lakṣmīpuṣṭisamanvitam
tathāṣṭādaśa dhānyāni bhājanāsanacandanaiḥ
dīpikopānahacchatra darpaṇaṃ pādukānvitam
dhvaje tu garuḍaṃ kuryāt kūbarāgre vināyakam
nānāphalasāmāyuktamupariṣṭādvitānakam
kauśeyaṃ pañcavarṇantu amlānakusumānvitam
caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ sārddhaṃ gaubhiraṣṭābhiranvitam
caturbhirhemamātaṅgaiḥ muktādāma vibhūṣitaiḥ
svarūpataḥ karibhyāñca yuktaṃ kṛtvā nivedayet
kuryāt pañcapalādūrdhvamābhārādapi śaktitaḥ
tathā maṅgalaśabdena snāpito vedapuṅgavaiḥ
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhīta kusumāñjaliḥ
namo namaḥ śaṅkarapadmajārka lokeśavidyādhara vāsudevaiḥ
vidyādharāmaramunīndragaṇābhijuṣṭaṃ prāpnotyasau padamatīndriyamindumauleḥ
nayati sa pitṛputrān bāndhavānapyaśeṣān kṛtagajarathadānācchāśvataṃ sadma viṣṇoḥ
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
pañcalāṃgalakaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya yugādi grahaṇādikām
bhūmidānaṃ naro dadyāt pañcalāṅgalakānvitam
kharvaṭaṃ kheṭakaṃ vāpi grāmaṃ vā sasyaśālinam
nivartanaśataṃ vāpi tadardhaṃ vāpi śaktitaḥ
sāradārumayān kṛtvā halān pañcavicakṣaṇaḥ
sarvopakaraṇairyuktānanyān pañca ca kāñcanān
vṛṣān lakṣaṇasaṃyuktān daśacaiva dhurandharān
suvarṇaśrṛṅgābharaṇān muktā lāṃgūla bhūṣaṇān
rūpyapādāgratilakān raktakauśeya bhūṣaṇān
sragdāmacandanayutān śālāyāmadhivāsayet
palāśasamidhastadvadājyaṃ kṛṣṇatilāstathā
tulāpuruṣavatkuryyāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
tato maṅgalaśabdena śuklamālyāmbaro budhaḥ
āhūya dvijadāmpatyaṃ hemasūtrāṃgulīyakaiḥ
śayyāṃ sopaskarāṃ dadyāddhenumekāṃ payasvinīm
tathāṣṭādaśadhānyāni samantādadhivāsayet
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
yasmāddevagaṇāḥ sarve sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca
dhurandharāṅge tiṣṭhanti tasmād bhaktiḥ śive'stu me
yasmācca bhūmidānasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm
daṇḍena saptahastena triṃśaddaṇḍaṃ nivartanam
tribhāgahīnaṃ gocarma mānamāha prajāpatiḥ
mānenānena yo dadyān nivartanaśataṃ budhaḥ
vidhinānena tasyāśu kṣīyate pāpasaṃhatiḥ
yāvanti lāṅgalakamārgamukhāni bhūmeḥ bhāsāṃpaterduhituraṅgaja romakāṇi
tāvanti śaṅkarapure sa samā hi tiṣṭet bhūmipradānamiha yaḥ kurute manuṣyaḥ
saṃpūjyate pitṛpitāmahabanduyuktaḥ śambhoḥ padaṃ vrajati cāmaranāyakaḥ san
athātaḥ saṃbhavakṣayāmi dharādānamanuttamam
pāpakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇāmamaṅgalyavināśanam
kārayetputrivīṃ haimīṃ jambūdvīpāṃnukariṇīm
lokapālāṣṭakopetāṃ navavaṣesamanvitām
nadīnadasamopetāṃ saptasāgaraveṣṭitām
mahāratnasabhākīrṇāṃ vasuradrākasaṃyutām
śatatrayeṇa vā kuryādviśatena śatena vā
kuryātpañcapalādrurdhvamaśakto'pi vicakṣaṇaḥ
tulāpuruṣavatkuryādvihoktāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
ṛtvijjaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
vedyāṃ kṛṣṇārjinaṃ kṛtvā tilānupari vinyaset
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasāṃśca lavaṇādikān
tathā'ṣṭau pūrṇakalaśānsamantātparikalpayet
vitānakaṃ ca kauśeyaṃ phalāni vividhāni ca
tathā'śukrāni ramyāṇi śrīkhaṇḍaśakalāni ca
ityevaṃ kārayitvā tāmadhivāsanapūrvakam
śukprālyāmbaradharaḥ śuktābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
puṇyaṃ kālamathā''sādya mantrānetānudīrayet
dhātrī tvaṃ sarvabhūtānāmataḥ pāhi vasudhare
vasudharā tato jātā tasmātpāhi bhaṃyādalam
anantāryai namastasmātpāhi saṃsārakarmamāt
tvameva lakṣmīrgovinde śive gaurīti cā''sthitā gāyatrī brahmaṇaḥ pārśve jyotsnā candre ravī prabhā
buddhirbṛhaspatī hyatā medhā muniṣu saṃsthitā viśvaṃ vyāpya sthitā yasmāttato viśvaṃbharā smṛtā
dhṛtiḥ sthitiḥ kṣamā kṣoṇī pruthvī vasumatī rasā
etābhirmurtibhiḥ pāhi devi saṃsārasāgarāt
evamucyārthaṃ tāṃ devīṃ brāhmaṇyo nivedayet
śeṣaṃ caivātha ṛtvigbhyaḥ prāṇipatya visarjayet
anena vidhinā yastu dadyāddemadharāṃ śubhām
puṇyakāle tu saṃprāpte sa padaṃ yāti vaiṣṇavam
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
nārāyaṇapuraṃ gatvā kalpatrayamathā''vaset
pitṛnputrāṃśca pautrāṃśca tārayedekaviṃśatim
iti paṭhati ya itthaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti prasaṅgādapi kaluṣavitānairmuktadehaḥ samantāt
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
viśvacakramitikhyātaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam
tapanīyasya śuddhasya viṣuvādiṣu kārayet
tasyārddhena kaniṣṭhaṃ syāt viśvacakramudāhṛtam
ṣoḍaśāraṃ tataścakraṃ bhramannemyaṣṭakāvṛtam
nābhipadme sthitaṃ viṣṇuṃ yogārūḍhaṃ caturbhujam
śaṅkacakre'sya pārśve tu devyaṣṭaka samāvṛtam
dvitīyāvaraṇe tadvat pūrvato jalaśāyinam
atrirbhṛgurvaśiṣṭhaśca brahmā kaśyapa eva ca
matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca narasiṃho'tha vāmanaḥ
rāmo rāmaśca kṛṣṇaśca buddhaḥ kalkīti ca kramāt
tṛtīyāvaraṇe gaurī mātṛbhirvasubhiryutā
caturthe dvādaśādityā vedāścatvāra eva ca
pañcame pañcabhūtāni rudrāścaikādaśaiva tu
lokapālāṣṭakaṃ ṣaṣṭe diṅmātaṅgāstathaiva ca
saptame'strāṇi sarvāṇi maṅgalānica kārayet
ṛtvigmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
viśvacakraṃ tataḥ kuryāt kṛṣṇājina tilopari
tathāṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasāṃśca lavaṇādikān
pūrṇakumbhāṣṭakañcaiva vastrāṇi vividhāni ca
mālyekṣuphalaratnāni vitānañcāpi kārayet
tato maṅgalaśabdena snātaḥ śuklāmbaro gṛhī
homādhivāsanānte vai gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
paramānandarūpī tvaṃ pāhinaḥ pāpakardamāt
hṛdi tatvaṃ guṇātītaṃ viśva cakraṃ namāmyaham
anyonyādhārarūpeṇa praṇamāmi sthitāviha
āyudhañcāpi vāsaśca bhavāduddhara māmataḥ
vaikuṇṭhalokamāsādya caturbāhuḥ sanātanaḥ
praṇamed dvādaśa kṛtvā viśvacakraṃ dine dine
śubhadarśanatāṃ prayāti śatrormadanasudarśanatāñca kāminībhyaḥ
sa sudarśanakeśavānurūpaḥ kanakasudarśanadānadagdhapāpaḥ
kṛtaguruduritāni ṣoḍaśārapravitaraṇe pravarākṛtirmurāreḥ
athātaḥ sapravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
mahākalpalatā nāma mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇa vācanam
ṛtvigmaṇḍapasambhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
tulā puruṣavat kuryāt lokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
cāmīkaramayīḥ kuryāddaśakalpalatāḥ samāḥ
nānāpuṣpaphalopetā nānāṃśuka vibhūṣitāḥ
vidyādharasuparṇānāṃ mithunairupaśobhitāḥ
hārānāditsubhiḥ siddhaiḥ phalāni ca vihaṅgamai
lokapālānukāriṇyaḥ kartavyāstāsu devatāḥ
ibhāsanasthā tu guḍe pūrvataḥ kuliśāyudhā
rajanī saṃsthitāgnāyī śruvapāṇirathānale
yāmye ca mahiṣārūḍhā gadinī taṇḍulopari
ghṛte tu nairṛtī sthāpyā sakhaṅgā dakṣiṇāpare
vāruṇe vāruṇī kṣīre jhaṣasthā nāgapāśinī
patākinī ca vāyavye mṛgasthā śarkaropari
saumyā tileṣu saṃsthāpyā śaṅkhinī nidhisaṃsthitā
māheśvarī vṛṣārūḍhā navanīte triśūlinī
maulinyo varadā tadvat kartavyā bālakānvitāḥ
śaktyā pañcapalādūrdhvamāsahasrāt prakalpayet
sarvāsāmupari sthāpyaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam
tato maṅgalaśabdena snātaḥ śuklāmbaro budhaḥ
namo namaḥ pāpavināśinībhyo brahmāṇḍalokeśvarapālinībhyaḥ
āśaṃsitādhikya phalapradābhyo digbhyastathā kalpalatāvadhūbhyaḥ
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
saptasāgarakaṃ nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanam
puṇyaṃ dinamathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
tulā puruṣavat kuryāt lokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
ṛtvigmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kārayet saptakuṇḍāni kāñcanāni vicakṣaṇaḥ
prādeśamātrāṇi tathā ratnimātrāṇi vai punaḥ
kuryāt saptapalādūrdhvamāsahasrācca śaktitaḥ
saṃsthāpyāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇājina tilopari
prathamaṃ pūrayetkuṇḍaṃ lavaṇena vicakṣaṇaḥ
dvitīyaṃ payasā tadvat tṛtīyaṃ sarpiṣā punaḥ
ṣaṣṭhaṃ śarkarayā tadvat saptamaṃ tīrthavāriṇā
sthāpayellavaṇasthaṃ tu brahmāṇaṃ kāñcanaṃ śubham
śarkarāyāṃ nyasellakṣmīṃ jalamadhye tu pārvatīm
tato vāruṇahomānte snāpito vedapuṅgavaiḥ
triḥpradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mantrānetānudīrayet
namo vaḥ sarvasindhūnāmādhārebhyaḥ sanātanāḥ
jantūnāṃ prāṇadebhyaśca samudrebhyo namo namaḥ
kṣīrodakājyadadhimādhuralāvaṇekṣu sārāmṛtena bhuvanatrayajīvasaṅghān
pāpakṣayāmṛtavilepanabhūṣaṇāya lokasya bibhrati tadastu mamāpi lakṣmīḥ
iti dadāti rasāmṛtasaṃyutān śuciravismayavāniha sāgarān
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
puṇyaṃ dinamathāsādya tulā puruṣadānavat
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā tato dhenuṃ prakalpayet
bhūmau kṛṣṇājinaṃ kṛtvā lavaṇadroṇasaṃyutam
sthāpayet padmarāgāṇāmekāśīti mukhe budhaḥ
puṣparāgaśataṃ tadvaddhoṇāyāṃ parikalpayet
lalāṭe hematilakaṃ muktā phalaśataṃ dṛśoḥ
bhrūyuge vidrumaśataṃ śukrī karṇadvaye smṛtau
kāñcanānāni ca śraṅgāṇi śiro vajra śatātmakam
grīvāyāṃ netrapaṭakaṃ gomedakaśatānvitam
indranīlaśataṃ pṛṣṭe vaiḍhūryaśatapārśvake
khurā hemamayāḥ kāryāḥ pucchaṃ muktāvalīmayam
kuṅkumāni ca romāṇi raupyanābhi ca kārayet
kuryāccharkarayā jihvāṃ gomayañca guḍātmakam
pucchāgre cāmaraṃ dadyāt samīpe tāmradohanam
kuṇḍalāni ca haimāni bhūṣaṇāni ca śaktitaḥ
tathā dhānyāni sarvāṇi pādāścekṣumayāḥ smṛtāḥ
nānā phalāni sarvāṇi pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam
evaṃ viracanaṃ kṛtvā tadvaddhomādhivāsanam
ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāddhenumāmantrayettataḥ
guḍadhenuvadāvāhya idañcodāharettataḥ
tvāṃ sarvadevagaṇadhāma yataḥ paṭhanti rudrendrasūryakamalāsana vāsudevāḥ
yaḥ puṇyamāpya dinamatrakṛtopavāsaḥ pāpairvimuktatanureti padaṃ murāreḥ
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
mahābhūtaghaṭaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam
puṇyāṃ tithimathāsādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
ṛtvigmaṇḍapasambhāra bhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
tulā puruṣavat kuryāt lokeśāvāhanādikam
kārayetkāñcanaṃ kumbhaṃ mahāratnācitaṃ budhaḥ
prādeśādaṃgulaśataṃ yāvat kuryāt pramāṇataḥ
kṣīrājyapūritaṃ tadvat kalpavṛkṣasamanvitam
padmāsanagatāṃstatra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān
lokapālān mahendrāṃśca svasvavāhanamāsthitān
varāheṇoddhṛtāṃ tadvat kuryāt pṛthvīṃ sapaṅkajām
varuṇaṃcāsanagataṃ kāñcanaṃ makaropari
hutāśanaṃ meṣagataṃ vāyuṃ kṛṣṇamṛgāsanam
tathā kośādhipaṃ kuryāt mūṣikasthaṃ vināyakam
ṛgvedasyākṣasūtraṃ syādyajurvedasya paṅkajam
sāmavedasya vīṇāsyādveṇuṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset
purāṇavedo varadaḥ sākṣasūtra kamaṇḍaluḥ
paritaḥ sarvadhānyāni cāmarāsanadarpaṇam
pādukopānahacchatraṃ dīpikā bhūṣaṇāni ca
śayyāñca jalakumbhāṃśca pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam
namo vaḥ sarvadevānāmādhārebhyaścarācare
mahābhūtādhidevebhyaḥ śāntirastu śivaṃ mama
brahmāṇḍe sarvabhūteṣu tasmācchrīrakṣayāstu me
ṣoḍaśaitāni yaḥ kuryāt mahādānāni mānavaḥ
iha paṭhati ya itthaṃ vāsudevasya pārśve sasutapitṛkalatraḥ saṃśrṛṇotīha samyak
idānīṃ kalpanāmāni samāsāt kathayācyuta
kalpānāṃ kīrtanaṃ vakṣye mahāpātakanāśanam
rauravaḥ pañcamaḥ proktaḥ ṣaṣṭo deva iti smṛtaḥ
sadyo'tha navamaḥ proktaḥ īśāno daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ
tama ekādaśaḥ proktaḥ tathā sārasvataḥ paraḥ
trayodaśa udānastu gāruḍo'tha caturdaśaḥ
kaurmaḥ pañcadaśaḥ proktaḥ paurṇamāsyāmajāyata
ṣoḍaśo nārasiṃhastu samānastu tato'paraḥ
āgneyo'ṣṭādaśaḥ proktaḥ somakalpastathāparaḥ
mānavo viṃśatiḥ proktastat pumāniti cāparaḥ
vaikuṇṭhaścāparastadvallakṣmīkalpastathāparaḥ
caturviṃśatimaḥ proktaḥ sāvitrī kalpasaṃjñakaḥ
pañcaviṃśastato ghoro vārāhastu tato'paraḥ
pitṛkalpastathānte tu yā kuhūrbrahmaṇaḥ parā
ityevaṃ brahmaṇo māsaḥ sarvapātakanāśanaḥ
ādāveva hi māhātmyaṃ yasminyasya vidhīyate
saṅkīrṇāstāmasāścaiva rājasāḥ sātvikāstathā
rajastamo mayāstadvadete triṃśadudāhṛtāḥ
saṅkīrṇeṣu sarasvatyāḥ pitṛṇāṃ vyuṣṭirucyate
agneḥ śivasya māhātmyaṃ tāmaseṣu divākare
rājaseṣu ca māhātmyamadhikaṃ brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam
yasmin kalpe tu yat proktaṃ purāṇaṃ brahmaṇā purā
sātvikeṣvadhikaṃ tadvadviṣṇormāhātmyamuttamam
brāhmaṃ pādmamimaṃ yastu paṭet parvaṇi parvaṇi
yastu dadyādimān kṛtvā haimān parvaṇi parvaṇi
brahmaviṣṇupure vāsaṃ munibhiḥ pūjyate divi
munirūpāṃstataḥ kṛtvā dadyāt kalpān vicakṣaṇaḥ
purāṇasaṃhitā ceyaṃ tava bhūpa mayoditā
sarvapāpaharā nityamārogya śrīphalapradā
brahmasaṃvatsaraśatādekāhaṃ śaivamucyate
śivavarṣaśatādekaṃ nimeṣaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ viduḥ
yadā saviṣṇurjāgarti tadedaṃ ceṣṭate jagat
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati
ityuktvā devadeveśo matsyarūpī janārdanaḥ
paśyatāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata
svāntaraṃ pālayāmāsa mārtaṇḍakulavarddhanaḥ
purāṇaṃ sarvaśāsrāṇāṃ yadetanmūrdhni saṃsthitam
sūta uvāca
etadvaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yaduktaṃ viśvarūpiṇā
mātsyaṃ purāṇamakhilaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam
sāṃkhyaṃ śārīrakaṃ proktaṃ caturmukhamukhodbhavam
madanadvādaśī tadvallokapālābhipūjanam
manvantarāṇāmuddeśo vainyarājābhivarṇanam
pitṛvaṃśānukathanaṃ śrāddhakālastathaiva ca
kīrtanaṃ somavaṃśasya yayāticaritaṃ tathā
kārtavīryasya mahātmyaṃ vṛṣṇivaṃśānukīrtanam
bhṛguśāpastathā viṣṇorddaityaśāpastathaiva ca
kīrtanaṃ puruṣeśasya vaṃśo hautāśanastathā
vrataṃ nakṣatrasaṃkhyākaṃ mārtaṇḍaśayanaṃ tathā
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ tadvadrohiṇīcandrasaṃjñitam
taḍāgavidhimāhātmyaṃ pādapotsarga eva ca
tathānantatṛtīyā tu rasakalyāṇinī tathā
ārdrānandakarī tadvadvrataṃ sārasvataṃ punaḥ
uparāgābhiṣekaśca saptamīsnapanaṃ punaḥ
bhīmākhyā dvādaśī tadvadanaṅgaśayanaṃ tathā
aśūnyaśayanaṃ tadvattathaivāṅgārakavratam
saptamī saptakaṃ tadvadviśoka dvādaśī tathā
meru pradānaṃ daśadhā grahaśāntistathaiva ca
grahasvarūpakathanaṃ tathā śivacaturdaśī
tathā sarvaphalatyāgaḥ sūryavāravrataṃ tathā
saṃkrāntisnapanaṃ tadvadvibhūtidvādaśīvratam
ṣaṣṭivratānāṃ māhātmyaṃ tatā snānavidhikramaḥ
prayāgasya tu māhātmyaṃ sarvatīrthānukīrtanam
tatāntarikṣacāraśca dhruvamāhātmyameva ca
bhuvanāni surendrāṇāṃ tripurādhoṣaṇaṃ tathā
pitṛpiṇḍadamāhātmyaṃ manvantaravinirṇayam
vajrāṅgasya tu saṃbhūtiḥ tārakotpattireva ca
tārakāsuramāhātmyaṃ brahmadevānukīrtanam
pārvatī ṛṣisaṃvādastataivodvāhamaṅgalam
vārāṇasyāstu māhātmyaṃ narmadāyāstathaiva ca
tatobhayamukhīdānaṃ dānaṃ kṛṣṇājinasya ca
tathā sāvitryupākhyānaṃ rājadharmāstathaiva ca
vāmanasya tu māhātmyaṃ tathaivātha varāhajam
kṣīrodamathanaṃ tadvat kālakūṭābhiśāsanam
prāsādalakṣaṇaṃ tadvat maṇḍapānāntu lakṣaṇam
kalpānukīrtanaṃ tadvad granthānukramaṇīṃ tathā
tulā dānādi bahuśo mahādānānukīrtanam
kalpānukīrtanaṃ tadvat grantānukramaṇīṃ tathā
etatpavitraṃ kalyāṇaṃ mahāpāpaharaṃ śubham
asmāt purāṇādapi pādamekaṃ paṭettu yaḥ so'pi vimuktapāpaḥ
nārāyaṇākhyaṃ padameti nūnamanaṅgavaddivyasukhāni bhuṅkte
samāptamidaṃ śrīmatsyamahāpurāṇam
